=== 2. parachute_mutations.db schema ===
CREATE TABLE source_snapshots (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            file_path TEXT NOT NULL,
            sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,
            content TEXT NOT NULL,
            created_ts DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        );
CREATE TABLE sqlite_sequence(name,seq);
CREATE TABLE source_mutations (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            file_path TEXT NOT NULL,
            mutation_name TEXT NOT NULL,
            before_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,
            after_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,
            success INTEGER NOT NULL,
            created_ts DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        );
CREATE TABLE parse_checks (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            file_path TEXT NOT NULL,
            parser TEXT NOT NULL,
            return_code INTEGER NOT NULL,
            output TEXT,
            created_ts DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        );
CREATE TABLE issues (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')), symptom TEXT NOT NULL, root_cause TEXT NOT NULL, fix_applied TEXT NOT NULL, verified INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0);
CREATE TABLE screenshots (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')), filename TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE, path TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE parse_errors (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')), script TEXT NOT NULL, error_text TEXT NOT NULL, fix_script TEXT NOT NULL, fix_result TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending');
CREATE TABLE game_state_log (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            run_id INTEGER,
            timestamp DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            game_state INTEGER,
            altitude REAL,
            is_deployed BOOLEAN,
            error TEXT
        );
CREATE TABLE omissions (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
            omission_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
            file TEXT NOT NULL,
            symptom TEXT NOT NULL,
            root_cause TEXT NOT NULL,
            fix_applied TEXT NOT NULL,
            fix_status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
            verified INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0);
CREATE TABLE resource_contention_log (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            session_id TEXT,
            godot_pid INTEGER,
            ts INTEGER,
            vm_rss_kb INTEGER,
            vol_cs INTEGER,
            nonvol_cs INTEGER,
            created_ts DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        , vol_ctxt_sw INTEGER, nonvol_ctxt_sw INTEGER, cpu_pct REAL, io_wait_pct REAL, sched_policy TEXT);
CREATE TABLE runtime_failures (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    run_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    failure_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    symptom TEXT NOT NULL,
    root_cause TEXT NOT NULL,
    fix_applied TEXT,
    verified INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);
CREATE TABLE asset_validation (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    asset_path TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_exists INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    run_id INTEGER NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE verbatim_logs (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                    run_id INTEGER,
                    command TEXT NOT NULL,
                    output TEXT NOT NULL,
                    exit_code INTEGER,
                    created_ts TEXT NOT NULL,
                    FOREIGN KEY (run_id) REFERENCES pipeline_runs(run_id)
                );
CREATE TABLE throttle_log (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                    operation TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                    attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                    last_attempt TEXT,
                    backoff_seconds INTEGER DEFAULT 30,
                    success_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                    failure_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                    locked INTEGER DEFAULT 0
                );
CREATE TABLE file_splits (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                original_file TEXT NOT NULL,
                split_file TEXT NOT NULL,
                split_index INTEGER NOT NULL,
                line_start INTEGER NOT NULL,
                line_end INTEGER NOT NULL,
                created_ts TEXT NOT NULL
            );
CREATE TABLE syntax_errors (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                file_path TEXT NOT NULL,
                line_number INTEGER NOT NULL,
                error_pattern TEXT NOT NULL,
                fix_applied TEXT,
                fixed_ts TEXT NOT NULL
            , error_text TEXT, code_line TEXT, context TEXT);
CREATE TABLE syntax_fixes (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                file_path TEXT NOT NULL,
                line_number INTEGER,
                pattern TEXT NOT NULL,
                fix_applied TEXT NOT NULL,
                fixed_ts TEXT NOT NULL
            );
CREATE TABLE error_patterns (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                pattern TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                replacement TEXT NOT NULL,
                description TEXT NOT NULL,
                priority INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
                file_filter TEXT DEFAULT '*.gd',
                enabled INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
                created_ts TEXT NOT NULL
            );
CREATE TABLE files_to_fix (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                file_path TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                line_number INTEGER,
                error_text TEXT,
                error_pattern_id INTEGER,
                before_content TEXT,
                after_content TEXT,
                status TEXT DEFAULT 'pending',
                attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                max_attempts INTEGER DEFAULT 3,
                last_attempt TEXT,
                fixed_ts TEXT,
                FOREIGN KEY (error_pattern_id) REFERENCES error_patterns(id)
            );
CREATE TABLE pipelineruns (
            run_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
            started_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            ended_at TEXT,
            status TEXT NOT NULL,
            total_processed INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            total_fixes INTEGER DEFAULT 0
        );
CREATE TABLE autohealactions (
            id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
            run_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            iteration INTEGER NOT NULL,
            file_path TEXT NOT NULL,
            tool_used TEXT NOT NULL,
            success INTEGER NOT NULL,
            timestamp TEXT NOT NULL, raw_output TEXT,
            FOREIGN KEY (run_id) REFERENCES pipelineruns(run_id) ON DELETE CASCADE
        );
CREATE TABLE gd_patch_registry (
            id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
            file TEXT NOT NULL,
            content_hash_before TEXT NOT NULL,
            content_hash_after TEXT NOT NULL,
            applied_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            UNIQUE(file, content_hash_after)
        );
CREATE TABLE verificationresults (
            id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
            run_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            component TEXT NOT NULL,
            passed INTEGER NOT NULL,
            verified_at TEXT NOT NULL, evidence TEXT,
            FOREIGN KEY (run_id) REFERENCES pipelineruns(run_id) ON DELETE CASCADE
        );
CREATE TABLE applied_fixes (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            fix_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
            file_path TEXT NOT NULL,
            fix_name TEXT NOT NULL,
            before_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,
            after_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,
            applied_at DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            run_id INTEGER,
            status TEXT DEFAULT 'applied',
            error_pattern TEXT,
            FOREIGN KEY (run_id) REFERENCES pipelineruns(run_id)
        );
CREATE TABLE repair_metrics (

    repair_rule_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,

    attempts INTEGER DEFAULT 0,

    successes INTEGER DEFAULT 0,

    failures INTEGER DEFAULT 0,

    success_rate REAL DEFAULT 0,

    FOREIGN KEY(repair_rule_id)

        REFERENCES repair_rules(id)

);
CREATE TABLE repair_evidence (

    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,

    repair_attempt_id INTEGER,

    stdout TEXT,

    stderr TEXT,

    exit_code INTEGER,

    created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,

    FOREIGN KEY(repair_attempt_id)

        REFERENCES repair_attempts(id)

);
CREATE TABLE schema_migrations (
            version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
            filename TEXT NOT NULL,
            applied_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            checksum TEXT DEFAULT 'bootstrap'
        );
CREATE TABLE auto_heal_actions (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                timestamp TEXT,
                tool TEXT,
                action TEXT,
                file_path TEXT,
                line_number INTEGER,
                before_line TEXT,
                after_line TEXT,
                success INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                error_message TEXT,
                iteration INTEGER DEFAULT 0
            , action_name TEXT, status TEXT, old_sha256 TEXT, new_sha256 TEXT, error_msg TEXT, exit_code INTEGER, stdout TEXT, stderr TEXT, fix_applied TEXT, attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0);
CREATE TABLE pipeline_runs (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                run_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                start_time TEXT NOT NULL,
                end_time TEXT,
                status TEXT DEFAULT 'running',
                iteration_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                errors_found INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                errors_fixed INTEGER DEFAULT 0
            , project_root TEXT, total_files TEXT, files_fixed TEXT, files_failed TEXT, outcome TEXT);
CREATE TABLE error_fingerprints (
        id            INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        error_class   TEXT    NOT NULL,
        pattern       TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE,
        description   TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
        created_at    INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s','now') * 1000000000)
    );
CREATE TABLE parse_error_log (
        id              INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        timestamp       INTEGER NOT NULL,
        file_path       TEXT    NOT NULL,
        error_class     TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'UNKNOWN',
        error_line      INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        error_col       INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        raw_stderr      TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
        strategy_tried  TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
        resolved        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
    );
CREATE TABLE pipeline_metadata (
            key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
            value TEXT
        );
CREATE TABLE known_repairs (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, error_class TEXT NOT NULL, strategy_name TEXT NOT NULL, pattern_old TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', pattern_new TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', description TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', created_at INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s','now') * 1000000000), UNIQUE(error_class, strategy_name));
CREATE TABLE strategy_rewards (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, error_class TEXT NOT NULL, strategy_name TEXT NOT NULL, wins INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, total_trials INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, last_updated INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s','now') * 1000000000), UNIQUE(error_class, strategy_name));
CREATE TABLE replay_api_tests (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                test_name TEXT NOT NULL,
                timestamp INTEGER NOT NULL,
                passed BOOLEAN NOT NULL,
                expected TEXT,
                actual TEXT,
                citation TEXT
            );
CREATE TABLE repair_attempts (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                rule_id INTEGER,
                file_path TEXT,
                error_class TEXT,
                success BOOLEAN,
                timestamp INTEGER DEFAULT (strftime('%s','now')),
                FOREIGN KEY (rule_id) REFERENCES repair_rules(id)
            );
CREATE TABLE tool_audit (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                timestamp INTEGER NOT NULL,
                file_path TEXT NOT NULL,
                tool TEXT NOT NULL,
                exit_code INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT -1,
                stdout TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
                stderr TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
                duration_ms INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
            , sha_before TEXT, sha_after TEXT, mutated INTEGER);
CREATE TABLE repair_rules (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    error_class TEXT,
    strategy_name TEXT,
    pattern_old TEXT,
    pattern_new TEXT,
    trials INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    wins INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    last_used INTEGER
);
CREATE TABLE component_registry (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            source_repo TEXT NOT NULL,
            source_commit TEXT,
            relative_path TEXT NOT NULL,
            class_name TEXT,
            extends_class TEXT,
            sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,
            file_size INTEGER,
            ingested_at TEXT NOT NULL, is_live_path INTEGER,
            UNIQUE(source_repo, relative_path)
        );
CREATE TABLE component_relationships (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            parent_class TEXT NOT NULL,
            child_class TEXT NOT NULL,
            relationship_type TEXT NOT NULL,
            source_repo TEXT NOT NULL,
            evidence TEXT,
            ingested_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            UNIQUE(parent_class, child_class, relationship_type, source_repo)
        );
CREATE TABLE flight_controller_recon (

CREATE TABLE pilot_career (
    pilot_id        INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    name            TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    total_flight_minutes INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    total_flights   INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    total_landings  INTEGER DEFAULT 0,   -- successful game completions
    total_fixes     INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    rank            TEXT DEFAULT 'Student Pilot',
    created_at      TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);
CREATE TABLE ratings (
    rating_id       INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    code            TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    name            TEXT NOT NULL,
    description     TEXT,
    required_fixes  INTEGER DEFAULT 10,
    category        TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE pilot_ratings (
    pilot_id        INTEGER,
    rating_id       INTEGER,
    earned_at       TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    PRIMARY KEY (pilot_id, rating_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (pilot_id) REFERENCES pilot_career(pilot_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (rating_id) REFERENCES ratings(rating_id)
);
CREATE TABLE pilot_category_fixes (
    pilot_id INTEGER,
    category TEXT,
    count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    PRIMARY KEY (pilot_id, category),
    FOREIGN KEY (pilot_id) REFERENCES pilot_career(pilot_id)
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "flights" (
        flight_id       INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        pilot_id        INTEGER NOT NULL,
        start_time      TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        end_time        TIMESTAMP,
        duration_minutes INTEGER,
        outcome         TEXT CHECK(outcome IN ('landed','crashed','aborted')),
        bugs_encountered INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
        bugs_fixed      INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
        notes           TEXT,
        FOREIGN KEY (pilot_id) REFERENCES pilot_career(pilot_id)
    );
CREATE TABLE code_patterns (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            pattern_type TEXT NOT NULL,
            path_pattern TEXT NOT NULL,
            style_value TEXT NOT NULL,
            confidence REAL DEFAULT 1.0,
            last_updated TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            UNIQUE(pattern_type, path_pattern)
        );
CREATE TABLE missing_function_fixes (
    file_path TEXT,
    missing_function TEXT,
    source_file TEXT,
    fixed_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    PRIMARY KEY (file_path, missing_function)
);
CREATE TABLE function_index (
    function_name TEXT,
    file_path     TEXT,
    line_number   INTEGER,
    PRIMARY KEY (function_name, file_path)
);
CREATE TABLE gdeye_diag (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        file TEXT,
        line INTEGER,
        column INTEGER,
        end_line INTEGER,
        end_column INTEGER,
        message TEXT,
        rule TEXT,
        severity TEXT,
        captured_at TEXT
    );
CREATE TABLE hud_state (
                    key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    value TEXT NOT NULL,
                    updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                );
CREATE TABLE component_health_tests (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            component TEXT NOT NULL,
            passed BOOLEAN NOT NULL,
            details TEXT,
            tested_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        );
CREATE TABLE events_hardened (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                ts TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ','now')),
                run_id TEXT, source_db TEXT, source_table TEXT,
                kind TEXT, file_path TEXT, detail TEXT, content_hash TEXT
            );
CREATE TABLE remediation_queue (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                created_ts TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ','now')),
                file_path TEXT, line_number INTEGER, error_text TEXT,
                status TEXT DEFAULT 'pending', attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                fix_script TEXT, priority INTEGER DEFAULT 5
            );

);

--- ./audit_logs/ab_test.db ---
CREATE TABLE ab_test_runs (
                run_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                session_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                ready_time_ms INTEGER,
                vol_cs INTEGER,
                nvol_cs INTEGER,
                outcome TEXT,
                timestamp DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            );
CREATE TABLE sqlite_sequence(name,seq);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "resource_contention_log_old" (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                session_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                godot_pid INTEGER NOT NULL,
                ts INTEGER,
                io_wait INTEGER,
                vol_cs INTEGER,
                nvol_cs INTEGER,
                timestamp DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            );
CREATE TABLE resource_contention_log (
                        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                        session_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                        godot_pid INTEGER NOT NULL,
                        ts INTEGER,
                        io_wait INTEGER,
                        vol_cs INTEGER,
                        nvol_cs INTEGER,
                        timestamp DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                        FOREIGN KEY (session_id) REFERENCES ab_test_runs(session_id)
                    );
CREATE INDEX idx_ab_test_runs_session ON ab_test_runs(session_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_ab_test_runs_timestamp ON ab_test_runs(timestamp);
CREATE INDEX idx_contention_session ON resource_contention_log(session_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_contention_ts ON resource_contention_log(ts);
CREATE TABLE godot_log_entries (
                entry_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                run_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                session_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                timestamp TEXT,
                verbatim TEXT,
                raw_json TEXT,
                FOREIGN KEY (run_id) REFERENCES ab_test_runs(run_id),
                FOREIGN KEY (session_id) REFERENCES ab_test_runs(session_id)
            );
CREATE INDEX idx_log_entries_run ON godot_log_entries(run_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_log_entries_session ON godot_log_entries(session_id);
CREATE TABLE godot_state_events (
                event_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                run_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                session_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                timestamp TEXT,
                event_type TEXT,   -- e.g., 'ENTER', 'EXIT', 'STATE', 'ERROR', 'INFO'
                event_name TEXT,   -- e.g., '_load_character', 'LINE OVER'
                raw_message TEXT,
                FOREIGN KEY (run_id) REFERENCES ab_test_runs(run_id)
            );
CREATE INDEX idx_state_events_run ON godot_state_events(run_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_state_events_type ON godot_state_events(event_type);
CREATE TABLE alerts_sent (
                run_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                alert_type TEXT,
                sent_at DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                FOREIGN KEY (run_id) REFERENCES ab_test_runs(run_id)
            );
CREATE TABLE auto_heal_actions (
                action_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                run_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                error_pattern TEXT,
                file_path TEXT,
                line_number INTEGER,
                patch_applied TEXT,
                status TEXT,
                timestamp DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                FOREIGN KEY (run_id) REFERENCES ab_test_runs(run_id)
            );
CREATE TABLE fix_patterns (
                pattern_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                error_keyword TEXT,         -- e.g., 'null', 'indented block', 'undefined'
                fix_template TEXT,           -- e.g., 'add_null_check', 'fix_indentation', 'declare_variable'
                file_glob TEXT,              -- optional: only apply to certain files
                success_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                last_used DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            );
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX "idx_mend_ab_test_runs_session_id" ON "ab_test_runs"("session_id");

            );

            , error_text TEXT, code_line TEXT, context TEXT);

            );

                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                pattern TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                replacement TEXT NOT NULL,
                description TEXT NOT NULL,
                priority INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
                file_filter TEXT DEFAULT '*.gd',
                enabled INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
                created_ts TEXT NOT NULL
            );
CREATE TABLE files_to_fix (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                file_path TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                line_number INTEGER,
                error_text TEXT,
                error_pattern_id INTEGER,
                before_content TEXT,
                after_content TEXT,
                status TEXT DEFAULT 'pending',
                attempt_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                max_attempts INTEGER DEFAULT 3,
                last_attempt TEXT,
                fixed_ts TEXT,
                FOREIGN KEY (error_pattern_id) REFERENCES error_patterns(id)
            );
CREATE TABLE pipelineruns (
            run_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
            started_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            ended_at TEXT,
            status TEXT NOT NULL,
            total_processed INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            total_fixes INTEGER DEFAULT 0
        );
CREATE TABLE autohealactions (
            id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
            run_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            iteration INTEGER NOT NULL,
            file_path TEXT NOT NULL,
            tool_used TEXT NOT NULL,
            success INTEGER NOT NULL,
            timestamp TEXT NOT NULL, raw_output TEXT,
            FOREIGN KEY (run_id) REFERENCES pipelineruns(run_id) ON DELETE CASCADE
        );
CREATE TABLE gd_patch_registry (
            id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
            file TEXT NOT NULL,
            content_hash_before TEXT NOT NULL,
            content_hash_after TEXT NOT NULL,
            applied_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            UNIQUE(file, content_hash_after)
        );
CREATE TABLE verificationresults (
            id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
            run_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            component TEXT NOT NULL,
            passed INTEGER NOT NULL,
            verified_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            FOREIGN KEY (run_id) REFERENCES pipelineruns(run_id) ON DELETE CASCADE
        );
CREATE TABLE applied_fixes (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            fix_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
            file_path TEXT NOT NULL,
            fix_name TEXT NOT NULL,
            before_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,
            after_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,
            applied_at DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            run_id INTEGER,
            status TEXT DEFAULT 'applied',
            error_pattern TEXT,
            FOREIGN KEY (run_id) REFERENCES pipelineruns(run_id)
        );
CREATE TABLE repair_metrics (

    repair_rule_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,

    attempts INTEGER DEFAULT 0,

    successes INTEGER DEFAULT 0,

    failures INTEGER DEFAULT 0,

    success_rate REAL DEFAULT 0,

    FOREIGN KEY(repair_rule_id)

        REFERENCES repair_rules(id)

);
CREATE TABLE repair_evidence (

    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,

    repair_attempt_id INTEGER,

    stdout TEXT,

    stderr TEXT,

    exit_code INTEGER,

    created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,

    FOREIGN KEY(repair_attempt_id)

        REFERENCES repair_attempts(id)

);

            version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,

        );

                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

                after_line TEXT,

            , action_name TEXT, status TEXT, old_sha256 TEXT, new_sha256 TEXT, error_msg TEXT, exit_code INTEGER, stdout TEXT, stderr TEXT, fix_applied TEXT);

                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        id            INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

    );

        );

        );

        template_text TEXT NOT NULL,
        dry_run_diff TEXT NOT NULL,
        score REAL NOT NULL,

        FOREIGN KEY (failure_site_id) REFERENCES failure_sites(id)
    );

CREATE TABLE pending_fixes (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            failure_site_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
            pattern_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
            file_path TEXT NOT NULL,
            line_number INTEGER,
            error_class TEXT,
            candidate_source TEXT,
            candidate_line INTEGER,
            template_text TEXT,
            diff_text TEXT,
            score REAL,
            applied BOOLEAN DEFAULT 0,
            applied_at TEXT,
            FOREIGN KEY (failure_site_id) REFERENCES failure_sites(id),
            FOREIGN KEY (pattern_id) REFERENCES mined_patterns(id)
        );

CREATE INDEX ix_pending_applied ON pending_fixes(applied);

            );

                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,
                gate_name TEXT NOT NULL,
                severity TEXT CHECK (severity IN ('INFO','WARN','ERROR','CRITICAL')),
                description TEXT NOT NULL,
                status TEXT DEFAULT 'OPEN' CHECK (status IN ('OPEN','RESOLVED','IGNORED')),
                file_hash TEXT
            );

                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,

                notes TEXT
            );

                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

                description TEXT NOT NULL,           -- e.g., "Evidence file must exist"

                last_updated TEXT NOT NULL
            );

            );
